26:["$","p",null,{"children":"In addition to this hybrid loop, you can also chain together different optimizers. This is useful when you want a global optimizer to find a good set of angles, and then a more fine-tuned optimizer to follow a gradient to the best set of neighboring angles. In the case of computational fluid dynamics (CFD), the default optimization sequence produces the best results - but in the case of material deformation (MD), while the default provides good results, you can configure it further for problem-specific benefits.","className":"group paragraph-el text-text-primary mx-0"}]
